# Agents · 0-9 · part 5

999 entries, 3forge-config-writer to _role_charter_template. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **3forge-config-writer**: AMI configuration file writer. Generates or updates `.properties` files for any AMI component (Center, Web, Relay, WebBalancer, WebManager). Only writes overrides that the project actually requires — AMI runs out of the box with no changes. Use when a project needs custom ports, paths, auth, SSL, persistence directories, or plugin configuration. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/3forge-mcp/agents/3forge-config-writer.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-datasource-advisor**: AMI datasource and feedhandler advisor. Identifies the right integration pattern for connecting external data to AMI, generates CALL __ADD_DATASOURCE stubs for query-based connections, generates Relay feedhandler configuration for streaming/push feeds, and produces custom Java AmiFH/AmiFHBase class skeletons for proprietary data sources. Use when the user wants to connect a new data source to AMI, configure a feedhandler, or implement a custom Java push adapter. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/3forge-mcp/agents/3forge-datasource-advisor.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-layout-architect**: AMI layout architect and builder. Designs and generates .ami layout files — divider tree, datamodel architecture, panel logic, callbacks, and AMIScript. Does not generate styles. Use for all .ami layout generation tasks. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/3forge-layout-architect.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-layout-style**: AMI layout visual stylist. Applies or improves the aesthetic design of .ami layout files — color themes, amiStyle properties, styleSets, custom CSS, column color formulas, and HTML panel design. Use when the user wants to style, theme, or visually polish a layout. Can also be called by 3forge-layout-architect after the structural layout is complete. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/3forge-layout-style.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-reviewer**: AMI Script code reviewer. Use PROACTIVELY after writing or generating AMI code. Reads files and reviews against all AMI rules — syntax, context (Center vs Web), security, and style. For .ami layout files, validates JSON structure, layout schema (v5/v6), and embedded AMIScript. Reports findings by severity. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/opencode/.opencode/agents/3forge-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-runtime**: Real-time natural language interface to a live AMI deployment. Connects directly to the AMI MCP plugin (Java, port 33333) — no sidecar. Lets users query data, run SQL, inspect tables, manage components, deploy layouts, and interact with Center/Relay/Web via plain English. Delegates schema design to 3forge-sql-builder and layout generation to 3forge-layout-architect. Use when the user wants to talk to a running AMI instance. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/3forge-mcp/agents/3forge-runtime.md) · 4 copies)
- **3forge-sql-builder**: AMIDB schema builder. Writes config_schema.amisql and managed_schema.amisql files — tables, indexes, triggers, timers, stored procedures, and datasource management. Use when a schema file needs to be created or modified for a 3forge AMI deployment. ([3f-git/3forge-mcp](https://github.com/3f-git/3forge-mcp/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/3forge-sql-builder.agent.md) · 4 copies)

- **4_logic**: Fourth agent in the COBOL reverse engineering pipeline. Reads parser_artifact.json and data_artifact.json to perform deep logic extraction on every COBOL program. Traces execution paths through the control flow graph, resolves EVALUATE/IF/AT END branching conditions, and translates each paragraph's COBOL statements into structured, annotated pseudocode. Produces one logic JSON per program (program_logic/) and a combined logic_artifact.json. This is the primary input for the Rules Agent and the Diagram Agent. Must be run after 3_data and before 5_rules or 6_diagram. ([Udaradg/legacy-modernization-harness](https://github.com/Udaradg/legacy-modernization-harness/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/4_logic.agent.md))

Traces execution paths through the control flow graph, resolves EVALUATE/IF/AT END branching conditions, and translates each paragraph's COBOL statements into structured, annotated pseudocode. Produces one logic JSON per program (program_logic/) and a combined logic_artifact.json. This is the primary input for the Rules Agent and the Diagram Agent. Lee el plan y el código implementado, aplica checks DDD + arquitectura hexagonal y produce el reporte de análisis. Es la PRIMERA parte del proceso de validación — su output es el insumo para @4b-validator-report. ([arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-backend](https://github.com/arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-backend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/4a-validator-analyze.md))
- **4a-validator-analyze**: Agente de análisis de validación para Arquisoft Frontend. Invocar cuando el usuario pida validar o analizar una implementación de HU/HT del cliente web. Valida en dos capas — sensores deterministas (tipos, tests incluido el de arquitectura, build, formato) y revisión con juicio contra las skills de arquitectura y estándares, que cubre lo que un test no ve — y produce el reporte sin persistirlo. Primera parte del proceso — su output es el insumo para @4b-validator-report. ([arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-frontend](https://github.com/arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-frontend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/4a-validator-analyze.md))
- **4b-validator-report**: Agente de persistencia del reporte de validación (parte 2 de 2). Invocar SOLO después de que @4a-validator-analyze haya producido un análisis APROBADO o RECHAZADO. Recibe el contenido del análisis y lo persiste en .workspace/validator/validator-{HU|HT}-{ID}.md, actualizando la fila Validación del plan. NO analiza, NO compila, NO hace commits — solo persiste lo que ya fue analizado. ([arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-backend](https://github.com/arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-backend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/4b-validator-report.md))
- **4b-validator-report**: Agente de persistencia del reporte de validación de frontend (parte 2 de 2). Invocar SOLO después de que @4a-validator-analyze haya producido un análisis APROBADO o RECHAZADO. Recibe el contenido del análisis y lo persiste en .workspace/validator/validator-{HU|HT}-{ID}.md, actualizando la fila Validación del plan. NO analiza, NO compila, NO ejecuta git — solo persiste lo que ya fue analizado. ([arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-frontend](https://github.com/arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-frontend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/4b-validator-report.md))
- **4c-commit**: Agente de entrega. Invocar manualmente después de que @4b-validator-report haya persistido un reporte APROBADO en .workspace/validator/. Ejecuta la cadena completa de entrega — commit, push, Pull Request hacia develop con la plantilla de .github, y publicacion del plan y el reporte de validacion en arquisoft-docs — con dos confirmaciones explícitas del usuario. No escribe código, no valida. ([arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-backend](https://github.com/arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-backend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/4c-commit.md))
- **4c-commit**: Agente de entrega de Arquisoft Frontend. Invocar después de que @4b-validator-report haya persistido un reporte APROBADO, o para subir cambios adicionales a una rama con PR ya abierto. Sin pedir confirmación ejecuta commit, push y Pull Request hacia develop con la plantilla de .github (con PR abierto, solo commit y push) y publica el plan y el reporte en arquisoft-docs con un commit directo a su main (sin PR). No escribe código, no valida. ([arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-frontend](https://github.com/arquisoft-uco/arquisoft-frontend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/4c-commit.md))

([undark-ai/Meta-Ads-OS](https://github.com/undark-ai/Meta-Ads-OS/blob/HEAD/agents/90-frequency-by-audience-agent.md))

- **93-conceive-scenario**: このagentは `.ai/skills/92-build-scenario/SKILL.md`（フロー外のシナリオ追加スキル）の**シナリオ着想の段**で使います。1つの入力（障害対応手順書 `ops/&lt;番号&gt;.md` / `INCIDENTS.tsv` の1行）から、関連する要求・仕様・設計・インシデント対応マニュアルを精査し、机上演習に使えるシナリオを1つ着想します。あわせて、既存シナリオとの重複を判定します。 ([Kuniwak/ttx](https://github.com/Kuniwak/ttx/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/93-conceive-scenario.md) · ★8)

([undark-ai/Meta-Ads-OS](https://github.com/undark-ai/Meta-Ads-OS/blob/HEAD/agents/94-prospecting-audience-and-creative-fit-agent.md))

- **96-prospecting-scale-readiness**: Runs Meta audit agent 96: whether prospecting can absorb more budget profitably, and how much, given audience headroom, diminishing returns and creative supply. - **98-retargeting-economics-and-over-retargeting**: Runs Meta audit agent 98: what retargeting costs and returns once existing customers and would-have-bought-anyway conversions are separated out, and where the account is paying to reach people it already reaches. - **99b65148-4d81-413f-9a2e-dacc584ae892**: 나는 손자, 『손자병법』 13편을 저술한 춘추시대의 병법가이자 인프라보안 분야을 이끄는 리더다. ([ccivlcid/AgentDesk](https://github.com/ccivlcid/AgentDesk/blob/HEAD/prompts/agents/99b65148-4d81-413f-9a2e-dacc584ae892.md) · ★5)
- **9_convener**: Fixed-voice pragmatic-chairperson persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; responds to each attacker's challenge against Team 8's seed story with either a rebuttal grounded in the seed story or ground-truth inputs, or a revision of the challenged section, itself subject to evidence_discipline. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_convener.md))
- **9_it_engineer**: Fixed-voice hands-on-practitioner persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; attacks Team 8's seed story's method and experimentation sections for underestimated implementation burden or a precondition the repository profile does not actually satisfy. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_it_engineer.md))
- **9_narrator**: Fixed-voice clear-technical-writer persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; invoked exactly once after all six section cycles close, to turn this run's working document and the full attack-defend transcript into the final practical research narrative document. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_narrator.md))
- **9_professor**: Fixed-voice senior-authority persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; attacks weak theoretical grounding, unsupported leaps from background to goal, and drift toward 0_problem_framing's out_of_scope boundaries in Team 8's seed story, then confirms whether the convener's response actually resolved its own challenge. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_professor.md))
- **9_scout**: Fixed-voice resourceful-field-researcher persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; hunts benchmark leaderboards, GitHub repos, and HuggingFace model/dataset pages for practical evidence to challenge or help defend Team 8's seed story, scoped to evidence the current story needs - never a re-run of Team 2/5's literature survey. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_scout.md))
- **9_student**: Fixed-voice naive-but-persistent persona for the Practical Research Narrative Team; asks the basic clarifying question an expert glosses over in Team 8's seed story's method and conclusion sections, catching blind spots professor's authority-based attack does not. ([tim-nish/PRISM](https://github.com/tim-nish/PRISM/blob/HEAD/agents/9_student.md))
- **9bow-2026-gstack-claude-code-virtual-team**: 이 글은 PyTorch KR 커뮤니티의 9bow(박정환)가 Garry Tan의 gstack을 한국어로 정리한 소개 글이다. gstack은 Y Combinator 대표가 공개한 오픈소스 software factory로, 글은 이를 "Claude Code를 사용하는 개발자가 20인 팀처럼 출시할 수 있도록" 설계된 28가지 슬래시 커맨드 모음으로 소개한다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/9bow-2026-gstack-claude-code-virtual-team.md))
- **9bow-2026-headroom-ai-agent-context-compression**: PyTorch KR에 9bow(박정환)가 2026-06-29에 올린 Headroom 소개글이다. Headroom을 "AI 에이전트가 처리하는 입력 데이터를 LLM에 전달하기 전에 압축하는 계층"으로 규정하고, 그 특징을 세 가지로 정리한다. ContentRouter 기반 압축, local-first 설계, 다중 배포 모드다. ([sguys99/ai-wiki](https://github.com/sguys99/ai-wiki/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/9bow-2026-headroom-ai-agent-context-compression.md))
- **9router — fable-5**: Custom agent backed by local 9router on http://127.0.0.1:20128 using model_id fable-5. ([daniyal-abbassi/HamiSite](https://github.com/daniyal-abbassi/HamiSite/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/9router-agent.md.agent.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;/script&gt;&lt;i&gt;AW-XSS-MARKER-FMNAME&lt;/i&gt;**: $(New-Item -ItemType File -Path AW-EXEC-MARKER-FM.txt) javascript:void(0) ([toonight/Airworthy](https://github.com/toonight/Airworthy/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/hostile/.github/agents/hostile.agent.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_ARCHITECT&gt;&gt;**: Architectural analysis - component boundaries, design options with trade-offs, the impact of a planned change across &lt;&lt;COMPONENTS&gt;&gt;. Use before deciding HOW to build something; it recommends and never implements. Read-only. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/architect.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_ARCHITECT_DEEP&gt;&gt;**: The &lt;&lt;NAME_ARCHITECT&gt;&gt; at xhigh effort, for a review of a PROCESS before or after an irreversible step (&lt;&lt;IRREVERSIBLE_STEPS&gt;&gt;), and other such reviews when the owner asks. Gives GO/NO-GO with blockers, or the rule changes a finished process calls for; it recommends and never implements. Read-only. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/architect-deep.head.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_CI_WATCHER&gt;&gt;**: Waits for a given GitHub Actions run (run id, commit SHA or PR number) to finish and reports its conclusion with the names of failed jobs and steps. Use instead of polling CI from a main session. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/ci-watcher.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_IMPLEMENTER&gt;&gt;**: Implements a scoped, already-decided change in its own worktree - one commit per item, runs the relevant checks, opens a PR. Use for implementation work on the risk paths (&lt;&lt;RISK_SUMMARY&gt;&gt;) and anything not on &lt;&lt;NAME_IMPLEMENTER_SIMPLE&gt;&gt;'s path list; it never merges or deploys. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/implementer.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_IMPLEMENTER_SIMPLE&gt;&gt;**: Implements a SIMPLE, low-risk change on Sonnet - &lt;&lt;SIMPLE_SUMMARY&gt;&gt;, docs and READMEs, test data and fixtures, mechanical refactors with no behaviour change. Refuses and hands back anything on the risk list (&lt;&lt;RISK_SUMMARY&gt;&gt;). Never merges or deploys. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/implementer-simple.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_OPERATOR&gt;&gt;**: Ops on Haiku - follows or dispatches ONE deploy of &lt;&lt;PROJECT&gt;&gt; through its workflow (production only with the owner's go quoted in the brief), watches the run, health-checks the result and reads server logs (&lt;&lt;LOG_SOURCES&gt;&gt;), then returns at most 15 lines with the key error lines verbatim. Reports failures, never fixes them; never changes a server's state; never prints secrets. Use instead of watching deploys, running ssh or reading logs from a main session. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/operator.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_REVIEWER_DEPLOY&gt;&gt;**: Pre-deploy review of a range of already-reviewed PRs - only the deploy path (&lt;&lt;DEPLOY_SUMMARY&gt;&gt;) and the interactions between the PRs, not the code again. Use once before dispatching a deploy. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/reviewer-deploy.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_REVIEWER_MECHANICAL&gt;&gt;**: Mechanical checks of a branch or PR - linters, formatters, type checks, test suites, skip audits, docs-vs-code consistency, pinned counters. Use for any check a tool can decide, and as the one review of a PR with no code (docs, fixtures, test data); not for judging security or business logic. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/reviewer-mechanical.md))
- **&lt;&lt;NAME_REVIEWER_SECURITY&gt;&gt;**: Pre-merge review of a PR that touches the risk paths (&lt;&lt;RISK_SUMMARY&gt;&gt;). Use once per such PR, before merge. Read-only; confirms every safety claim by probing production entry points. ([Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents](https://github.com/Voyteck/claude-skill-project-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/project-agents/assets/agents/reviewer-security.md))
- **&lt;agent-name&gt;**: &lt;ONE precise sentence — WHEN to invoke. The router matches on this. - **&lt;agent-name&gt;**: &lt;One-line description of agent expertise and focus&gt; ([houke/nexus-playground](https://github.com/houke/nexus-playground/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/_template.md))
- **&lt;agent-name&gt;**: &lt;一句话描述&gt; ([KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config](https://github.com/KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config/blob/HEAD/home/.claude/agents/_template.md) · 2 copies)
- **&lt;agent-name&gt;**: &lt;one line: what this reviewer is expert in and when it should fire&gt; ([lucas-lima-s/claude-skill-layered-review](https://github.com/lucas-lima-s/claude-skill-layered-review/blob/HEAD/agents/_TEMPLATE.md))

- **&lt;agent-slug&gt;**: &lt;one-sentence description of what this specialist owns. Mention the surface (stack layer, SDK, feature area) plus the verb (implements, validates, deploys, ...). Tech-portable; no domain content.&gt; ([leamariek/aevum](https://github.com/leamariek/aevum/blob/HEAD/examples/agents/AGENT_TEMPLATE.md) · ★4)
- **&lt;agent-slug&gt;**: Domain entry agent for &lt;domain&gt;. First reader for any &lt;domain&gt;-related task — triages, routes deeper if specialists exist, produces domain-shaped response. Reports to the interface agent. ([DScardini91/agentic-os](https://github.com/DScardini91/agentic-os/blob/HEAD/control-plane/templates/agents/domain-entry.template.md) · ★2)
- **&lt;agent-slug&gt;**: Orchestrator for the &lt;domain&gt; committee — coordinates &lt;N&gt; specialists (&lt;list&gt;) into a single synthesis. Does not produce the underlying content; weighs and integrates. Reports to the interface agent. ([DScardini91/agentic-os](https://github.com/DScardini91/agentic-os/blob/HEAD/control-plane/templates/agents/orchestrator.template.md) · ★2)
- **&lt;agent-slug&gt;**: Entity guardian for &lt;thing being protected&gt;. Hard gate consulted whenever a proposal draws from the &lt;thing&gt; account. Read-only — surfaces concrete observations, does not block. Reports to the interface agent. ([DScardini91/agentic-os](https://github.com/DScardini91/agentic-os/blob/HEAD/control-plane/templates/agents/entity-guardian.template.md) · ★2)
- **&lt;agent-slug&gt;**: Catch-all for tasks no specialist owns. Executes the work and logs the invocation as a coverage-gap signal for the OS analyst. Reports to the interface agent. ([DScardini91/agentic-os](https://github.com/DScardini91/agentic-os/blob/HEAD/control-plane/templates/agents/fallback.template.md) · ★2)
- **&lt;agent-slug&gt;**: &lt;one line — WHEN to delegate to this agent, not what it is&gt; ([loctu0402/agent-analytic-package](https://github.com/loctu0402/agent-analytic-package/blob/HEAD/outputs/submissions/_TEMPLATE/aap-pack/.claude/agents/_AGENT_.example.md))
- **&lt;AGENT_NAME&gt;**: &lt;DESCRIPTION&gt; ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/blob/HEAD/agentic/code/frameworks/sdlc-complete/agents/agent-template.md) · ★230 · 2 copies)
- **&lt;Display Name Humano&gt;**: &lt;Frase única, clara, com gatilho. Quando esse agent ativa, o que ele entrega. Curto e específico.&gt; ([simpletibr/simplicio-local](https://github.com/simpletibr/simplicio-local/blob/HEAD/.github/copilot/agents/_template.agent.md) · ★18 · 2 copies)
- **&lt;generated-agent-name&gt;**: &lt;when-this-agent-needs-to-be-called&gt; ([ai-driven-dev/framework](https://github.com/ai-driven-dev/framework/blob/HEAD/cli/tests/fixtures/framework-real/plugins/aidd-context/skills/03-context-generate/assets/agents/agent-template.md) · ★566 · 2 copies)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;When to dispatch this agent. Be specific — the harness routes by this text. Include concrete trigger phrases the user might say. Lead with the capability, then "Use when…", then "Triggers: …".&gt; ([BrendenKennedy/claude-for-ai-platforms](https://github.com/BrendenKennedy/claude-for-ai-platforms/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_TEMPLATE.md) · ★2)
- **&lt;kebab-case; MUST equal the sibling skill folder this agent wraps&gt;**: &lt;one line — what this dispatched leaf does + the capability/orchestrator that fans out to it. Describe the dispatch relationship in prose only; an agent has no Dependencies edge and never names its orchestrator as one.&gt; ([ZachArticulateV/designer-pro-and-seo](https://github.com/ZachArticulateV/designer-pro-and-seo/blob/HEAD/agents/_TEMPLATE.md) · ★10)
- **&lt;kebab-case&gt;**: &lt;one sentence&gt; ([trustmybot/plugin](https://github.com/trustmybot/plugin/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/template.md) · ★6)
- **&lt;layer&gt;-specialist**: GENERATED ONCE PER LAYER of your implementation chain by /adapt-to-stack, into the repo's own .claude/agents/ (see docs/shared/11-adapting-to-your-stack.md). Executes the approved plan for its ONE layer. Reads planner.md + the upstream layer's contract handoff; implements its layer in the repo(s) it owns; runs the local build/tests; commits with amend-as-you-go (one commit per repo); writes its own contract handoff for the next layer. Never pushes. ([konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook](https://github.com/konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/layer-specialist.md) · ★3)
- **&lt;Member name&gt;**: You are **&lt;Member name&gt;**, a member of the round-table. ([cedricamram/claude-roundtable](https://github.com/cedricamram/claude-roundtable/blob/HEAD/agents/_TEMPLATE.md))
- **&lt;member-name&gt;**: &lt;one-line description of who this family member is, what they do, and what register they speak in&gt;. Their persistent state lives in family/family.db; their hash-chained action log lives in family/&lt;member-name&gt;_ledger.db. ([AetherLogosPrime-Architect/DivineOS](https://github.com/AetherLogosPrime-Architect/DivineOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/family-member-template.md) · ★6)
- **&lt;nome-do-papel&gt;**: &lt;Uma frase objetiva: em que situação este agente deve ser usado, e o que ele explicitamente NÃO deve fazer (para não sobrepor com outro papel).&gt; ([Senavictors/bootstrap-agent-architecture-plugin](https://github.com/Senavictors/bootstrap-agent-architecture-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugin/skills/bootstrap-init/assets/templates/claude/agents/_role-template.md) · ★5)
- **&lt;product name&gt;**: Run `/health-audit`. Reports go to `.flow/audits/`. ([scoutredeem/scaffolding](https://github.com/scoutredeem/scaffolding/blob/HEAD/ce/templates/agent-docs/agents/audit.md))
- **&lt;project&gt;-auditor**: Read-only review and research, such as security, performance, content or code reviews, with sources. Writes only its report under docs/reviews/ and never changes product code. ([DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy](https://github.com/DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/auditor.md))
- **&lt;project&gt;-docs**: Writes and edits documentation, such as specs, policy pages, logs and copy. Use for docs work that needs no code change. ([DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy](https://github.com/DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/docs-writer.md))
- **&lt;project&gt;-verifier**: Runtime verification at ladder levels L2 to L5, such as headless web screenshots, simulator checks, native builds and end-to-end flows. Fixes tests and end-to-end flows only, never product code. Use after an implementation agent finishes. ([DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy](https://github.com/DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/verifier.md))
- **&lt;project&gt;-web**: Implements and fixes one area of the &lt;project&gt; project, such as pages, styles, features and their tests. Use for work in that area only. ([DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy](https://github.com/DalatHappyFarm/agent-steddy/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/implementer.md))
- **&lt;redacted&gt;**: &lt;redacted&gt; ([guillermolg00/moldig](https://github.com/guillermolg00/moldig/blob/HEAD/fixtures/copilot/trust-and-sessions/root/project-a/.github/agents/planner.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-architect**: Use proactively for feature planning and cross-area impact analysis in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Invoke before implementation when a change touches more than one skill's applicability scope, changes shared contracts (proto / schema / public API), or adds new code-gen surface. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-architect.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-maintainer**: Use when a plan or concrete change set is already defined and needs to land in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Follows skill conventions, runs static analysis after each logical change set, and blocks on errors. Standard part of the orchestrated code↔review loop (`/&lt;repo&gt;-implement`). ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-maintainer.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-precommit-auditor**: Invoke via `/&lt;repo&gt;-audit` immediately before `git commit` in the &lt;project name&gt; repo. Read-only - audits staged changes for commit-readiness (debug prints, stale TODOs introduced in this change, dead comments, leftover scaffolding, AI-cruft comments) and convention drift visible only at the diff level. Complements `&lt;repo&gt;-reviewer` by focusing on commit-ready cleanliness, not code correctness. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-precommit-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;repo&gt;-reviewer**: Use after the maintainer completes a change set in the &lt;project name&gt; repo - reviews the diff from scratch and produces a classified BLOCKER / WARN / NIT list (correctness, regressions, missing tests, contract drift, convention violations). Read-only - fixes are applied by the maintainer or main context. ([Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills](https://github.com/Utopia-USS/utopia-flutter-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/utopia-ai-arch/skills/layer/templates/claude-layer/agents/REPO-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **&lt;reviewer-name&gt;**: Independent, read-only review of a &lt;Project&gt; change (uncommitted work, a commit, or a range) for correctness bugs, violations of AGENTS.md rules, and whether it meets the task's acceptance criteria. Use after implementation and testing, before commit or merge. Give it the task reference and the diff target. It never edits files. ([kanjicool/reum](https://github.com/kanjicool/reum/blob/HEAD/skills/kickoff/templates/agents/reviewer.md))
- **&lt;Role Name&gt;**: &lt;When to invoke&gt; ([gammelgaard1993-gif/zip01](https://github.com/gammelgaard1993-gif/zip01/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/template-for-agent.md))
- **&lt;slug-lowercase-hyphenated&gt;**: &lt;one-paragraph description that includes trigger phrases. Pattern - "Use when &lt;situation&gt;. Owns &lt;files/dirs&gt;. Triggers - 'literal user utterance 1', 'literal user utterance 2', 'literal user utterance 3'." Trigger phrases drive auto-selection - without them the agent will not be invoked.&gt; ([Enovatr-Labs/SpecRoute](https://github.com/Enovatr-Labs/SpecRoute/blob/HEAD/agents/agent-template.md) · ★3)
- **&lt;SPECIALIST_NAME&gt;**: &lt;When to spawn this agent. One sentence on what it does, one on what it does NOT do.&gt; ([daretodave/nexus](https://github.com/daretodave/nexus/blob/HEAD/templates/claude/agents/generic-specialist.md) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **&lt;SURFACE_AGENT&gt;**: Implements the &lt;SURFACE_LABEL&gt; surface (&lt;SURFACE_PATH&gt;) for one feature, strictly from the frozen spec + contract, test-first TDD. Dispatched by /cohorte-build. Touches only its own surface. ([TheBidouilleAgency/cohorte](https://github.com/TheBidouilleAgency/cohorte/blob/HEAD/core/agents/implementer.template.md) · ★2)
- **&lt;tester-name&gt;**: Tests a &lt;Project&gt; change against a task's acceptance criteria and returns a short pass/fail report with evidence. Use after implementing a task and before review or commit. Give it the task reference (or the exact criteria) and what changed. It never edits code. ([kanjicool/reum](https://github.com/kanjicool/reum/blob/HEAD/skills/kickoff/templates/agents/tester.md))
- **&lt;work&gt;-coordinator**: Coordinator for &lt;the work&gt; done by an outside agent (&lt;tool&gt;). Briefs it through a shared folder, reviews each batch with &lt;specialists&gt;, writes feedback, and brings accepted output into the repo. Never does the outside agent's work itself; never pushes or merges. ([jeremywight/graph-run](https://github.com/jeremywight/graph-run/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/coordinator.md))

- **[paraphraser](../../docs/vocabulary.md#def-paraphraser)**: Renders one [intermediate representation](../../docs/vocabulary.md#def-intermediate-representation) (IR) into prose for one catalog reader, applying the [style specification](../../docs/vocabulary.md#def-style-specification). Fresh context by design. Give it only the IR text, the reader entry and the style rows for that reader. It introduces no claim and drops none. ([majodali/communication-pipeline](https://github.com/majodali/communication-pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/paraphraser.md))
- **[Power Platform] ALM Engineer**: Assesses and implements healthy Power Platform ALM: environment strategy, solution architecture, source control, CI/CD pipelines, managed deployments, and operational governance. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ALMEngineer.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Average User**: Simulates a naive end-user performing usability tasks in a live browser session. Uses Playwright-based browser tools to interact with the UI, narrates what they see and do, and reports confusion, frustration, and dead ends. Used by Usability Engineer agent for structured usability studies. Use when running usability tests, simulating real user behavior, or validating whether a UI is intuitive without prior knowledge. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/AverageUser.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Code Reviewer**: Performs deep, systematic code review: finds duplication, dead code, anti-patterns, complexity issues, security vulnerabilities, and technical debt before they compound. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/CodeReviewer.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Consultant**: Orchestrator agent and single entry point for all user requests. Routes work to specialist agents and team skills, enforces human approval gates for immutable decisions, and maintains project-wide state. Delegates to subagents via runSubagent directly or via skills (team-planning, code-review) depending on task complexity. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/Consultant.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Data Modeler**: Designs data schemas for applications, defining tables, columns, relationships, option sets, and security models without writing code — producing schema design documents. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/DataModeler.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Developer**: Autonomous coding agent that implements features, fixes, and refactoring from plans. Persists state via workflow-state, validates work via browser-automation, and iterates until stable. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/Developer.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Solution Architect**: System architecture designer who selects patterns, evaluates technology choices, and ensures scalability and maintainability. Produces architecture design documents and recommendations without writing implementation code. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/SolutionArchitect.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Technical Writer**: Assesses documentation completeness, clarity, accuracy, and ensures developers, users, and operators have the information they need. Use when reviewing README files, API docs, onboarding guides, runbooks, or inline comments for gaps, inconsistencies, or unclear language. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/TechnicalWriter.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Tester**: Produces comprehensive test plans covering unit tests, integration tests, usability testing (via Usability Engineer), browser automation, large-dataset and edge-case scenarios, and mocking strategies. Assesses testability, identifies edge cases, defines quality gates, and ensures comprehensive test coverage before code reaches production. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/Tester.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] The Critic**: Challenges assumptions, finds blind spots, identifies unstated risks, and ensures thorough consideration of edge cases and alternatives. Used by team-planning skill. Use when evaluating plans, designs, or proposals before finalizing — especially for security, scalability, edge cases, and unstated assumptions. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/TheCritic.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] Usability Engineer**: Conducts structured usability studies by using runSubagent to delegate to the Average User agent with browser tasks, observing their behavior, and producing a professional usability report with severity-ranked findings. Use for usability testing, UX evaluation, user testing, heuristic evaluation, or UX audit. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/UsabilityEngineer.agent.md) · ★40)
- **[Power Platform] UX Designer**: Designs the user-facing structure of applications including personas, navigation, forms, views, user flows, and dashboards without writing code — producing UI/UX design documents. ([scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills](https://github.com/scottdurow/power-platform-full-stack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/UXDesigner.agent.md) · ★40)

([Growrix/Agency](https://github.com/Growrix/Agency/blob/HEAD/VSCODE%20AGENTS/Testing-Cursor_dev/Backend%20%26%20Deploy/.github/agents/phase1.3-replica-to-native-nextjs-frontend.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **[REPLI SYSTEM] Phase 1.4 HTML to Verified Native Next.js Frontend**: This agent repairs the failure mode where a generated Next.js app still depends on raw HTML source files, HTML parser rendering, public HTML ownership, generated dumps, or legacy script behavior while presenting itself as native. ([Growrix/Agency](https://github.com/Growrix/Agency/blob/HEAD/VSCODE%20AGENTS/Testing-Cursor_dev/Backend%20%26%20Deploy/.github/agents/phase1.4-html-to-verified-native-nextjs-frontend.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **[REPLI SYSTEM] Phase 1.4 HTML to Verified Native Next.js Frontend**: [REPLI SYSTEM][CANDIDATE] Use when migrating a raw HTML/static template folder or repairing a failed HTML-backed Next.js attempt into a separate verified pure native Next.js App Router frontend with route-by-route ownership proof, no parser/source-HTML runtime, native flows, visual parity, and zero Problems. - **[SubAgent] API Layer**: Builds the API layer for a .NET DDD aggregate — thin attribute-routed controllers, the Result-to-IActionResult filter, IMiddleware implementations, attribute filters, and Program.cs/DI wiring. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use to expose a use-case over HTTP or add cross-cutting HTTP pipeline behavior. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/api-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Application Layer**: Builds the Application layer for a .NET DDD aggregate — DTOs in the .DTO project, Result/Error-returning application services orchestrated through IUnitOfWork, and DI wiring. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use after the domain and repository contracts exist, before exposing it over HTTP. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/application-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Backlog Reviewer**: Reviews backlog items against INVEST and the Definition of Ready, proposes vertical splits for oversized stories, and produces relative estimate proposals anchored on a reference story. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use before an item is called ready, and during refinement of an existing backlog. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/scrum-agent/agents/backlog-reviewer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Bug Spike Author**: Writes Bug and Spike backlog items — observed vs. expected behavior with reproduction and impact, or one timeboxed question with a written answer as its deliverable. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use to log a defect, or to carve out an investigation that blocks an estimate or a decision. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/scrum-agent/agents/bug-spike-author.md))
- **[SubAgent] Domain Layer**: Writes the Domain layer for a .NET DDD API — entities deriving from BaseEntity, enums, Value Objects, domain-service contracts, and invariant/validation logic. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use when modeling a new aggregate's core or adding business rules. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/domain-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Epic Author**: Writes Epic-level backlog items — business outcome, hypothesis, success metrics, non-goals, and the list of child Features. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use when framing a large initiative before any feature or story exists. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/scrum-agent/agents/epic-author.md))
- **[SubAgent] Feature Author**: Writes Feature-level backlog items — the Azure DevOps level between Epic and User Story. Produces shippable capabilities with a value statement, scope boundary, feature-level acceptance and a child-story list. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use when decomposing an epic or when a request is too big to be one story. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/scrum-agent/agents/feature-author.md))
- **[SubAgent] Infrastructure Layer**: Writes the Infrastructure layer for a .NET DDD API — EF Core repositories, entity configurations, ApplicationDbContext, UnitOfWork, EF migrations, and resilient outbound HttpClients. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use for persistence wiring, data-access concerns, and calls to external HTTP services. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/dotnet-ddd/agents/infrastructure-layer.md))
- **[SubAgent] Story Author**: Writes User Story backlog items — the As a/I want/so that sentence, context, Gherkin acceptance criteria covering happy and unhappy paths, and explicit out-of-scope. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use to turn a feature into demonstrable vertical slices. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/scrum-agent/agents/story-author.md))
- **[SubAgent] Task Planner**: Breaks committed stories into implementation Tasks of one day or less and maintains the sprint file — goal, capacity, committed and stretch items, risks, and the results when it closes. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill as part of a plan. Use at sprint planning, after stories are ready and estimated, and when closing a sprint. ([jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework](https://github.com/jino-labs/AI-Agent-Framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/scrum-agent/agents/task-planner.md))
- **[SubAgent] Test Author**: Writes xUnit tests for a .NET DDD API, one test project per layer mirroring tests/unit-tests, plus HTTP pipeline tests. Invoked by the main conversation or a skill after another [SubAgent] finishes its layer — tests are mandatory for every new implementation, not optional. Use to cover domain rules, application services, repositories, controllers/filters/middlewares, and mappings. - **[SubAgent] Tracker Sync**: Projects the markdown backlog onto GitHub Issues and reconciles drift — runs the deterministic sync engine's plan phase and reports it, and applies only a batch the user already confirmed. - **[SubAgent] Transversal Layer**: Writes the Transversal layer for a .NET DDD API — entity-to-DTO Mapster mappings, and any future framework-agnostic cross-cutting concern. - **__README**: A comprehensive collection of specialized Claude Code agents for software development, designed as **expert consultants** that provide architectural guidance, decision frameworks, and best practices. Each agent focuses on helping you make informed technology choices and design decisions rather than generating code. - **_privacy**: Privacy Engineer specializing in reidentification risk, data minimization, retention enforcement, and anonymity architecture. Distinct from security — security asks whether an attacker can get in, privacy asks what the system reveals when everything works as designed. Trigger on anonymity, pseudonymity, reidentification, k-anonymity, retention, data minimization, PII, GDPR, CCPA, or privacy review. - **_probe_architect**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that a native subagent dispatched with `CEO_ARCHITECT_ACTIVE=1` env var is blocked from acting as a parallel CEO architect. Architect role must remain a CEO-only function — never delegated to a subagent. Expected hook output: `{"decision":"block","reason":"GOVERNANCE: architect_role_not_delegable: ..."}`. If the hook allows architect dispatch via native rail, role separation is broken. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_architect.md) · ★3)
- **_probe_canonical_edit**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that a native subagent attempting to Edit/Write a canonical-guarded path WITHOUT a sentinel approved.md is blocked by `check_canonical_edit.py`. Expected hook output: `{"decision":"block","reason":"CANONICAL-EDIT-BLOCKED: ..."}`. If the canonical hook allows the edit when invoked from the native rail, defense-in-depth is broken and Phase 1 is NO-GO. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_canonical_edit.md) · ★3)
- **_probe_missing_skill**: PLAN-020 Phase 0 item 5 governance probe — verifies that `check_agent_spawn.py` BLOCKS dispatch via the native subagent rail when the spawn prompt has no skill content (neither `## SKILL CONTENT` inline NOR `## SKILL REFERENCE`). Expected hook output for any Task tool call dispatching this agent: `{"decision":"block","reason":"GOVERNANCE: missing_skill_content: ..."}`. If the hook ALLOWS this dispatch, native rail has a governance gap and Phase 1 is NO-GO. ([Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration](https://github.com/Canhada-Labs/ceo-orchestration/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_probe_missing_skill.md) · ★3)

- **_protocol_CONTEXT_HYGIENE**: - **Auto-compact**: 80%で自動発動（`CLAUDE_AUTOCOMPACT_PCT_OVERRIDE=80`） - **手動compact**: 50%到達で推奨（`/compact`） - **Topic変更**: Activity Log記録 → `/clear` - **迷走検知**: 3回同じ失敗 → `/rewind` + 方針変更 - **長チェーン**: 5ステップ以上 → 中間compact（完了済み/残り/判断理由を`.agents/PROJECT.md`に記録） ([hinominant/cc-orchestration-workshop](https://github.com/hinominant/cc-orchestration-workshop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_protocol_CONTEXT_HYGIENE.md))

- **_protocol_SLIDE_KIND_CATALOG**: &gt; Design Harness Initiative Story 3 成果物。 &gt; 全プロジェクトで再利用可能な Slide kind 12 種 + Visual primitives 4 種を定義。 &gt; 各プロジェクトは本カタログを参照し、自プロジェクトの `components/slides/types.ts` / `primitives.tsx` に &gt; コピー or 部分採用してカスタマイズする。 ([hinominant/cc-orchestration-workshop](https://github.com/hinominant/cc-orchestration-workshop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_protocol_SLIDE_KIND_CATALOG.md))

- **_README**: Each file here defines the prompt for one isolated sub-agent. The orchestrator (`/component-create`) invokes them via the `Task` tool, one phase at a time. **A sub-agent sees only the prompt the orchestrator hands it** — no conversation history, no other sub-agent's output, no chat memory. This is the structural guarantee that "the agent never invents." ([aziontech/webkit](https://github.com/aziontech/webkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_README.md) · ★3)
- **_README**: Each file is a job description, not a prompt. Written against a ROLE, never a niche, so the same agent works for any focus. - **_reflective_debugging_guick_reference**: Think across these categories: - ✅ **Code Logic**: Algorithm bugs, edge cases, conditionals - ✅ **Data**: Schema mismatches, null values, type issues - ✅ **Config**: Environment vars, paths, versions - ✅ **Integration**: API contracts, timing, resources - ✅ **LLM** (if applicable): Prompt clarity, context, examples - ✅ **State**: Cache staleness, initialization, corruption - ✅ **External**: Servic ([imvladikon/dot-claude](https://github.com/imvladikon/dot-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/_reflective_debugging_guick_reference.md) · ★3)

- **_review-protocol**: **Two gates, one contract.** At **Gate 1** several agents read the same feature spec; at **Gate 1.5** several read the same design. In both, each asks only the questions its role is qualified to ask, and in both you produce **findings, not artifacts** — you write no files at all. ([tandt53/todo-ai-redesign](https://github.com/tandt53/todo-ai-redesign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_review-protocol.md))
- **_review_design**: Use when: reviewing code for design and data structure choices, algorithmic efficiency, whether the right abstractions are used, representation trade-offs, API surface design. ([ihmeuw/vivarium-suite](https://github.com/ihmeuw/vivarium-suite/blob/HEAD/tools/ai-tools-public/agents/_review_design.md) · ★4)
- **_review_documentation**: Use when: reviewing code for documentation quality, including docstrings, comments, README/changelog updates, and public API documentation accuracy. ([ihmeuw/vivarium-suite](https://github.com/ihmeuw/vivarium-suite/blob/HEAD/tools/ai-tools-public/agents/_review_documentation.md) · ★4)
- **_review_dry**: Use when: reviewing code for DRY violations, duplicated logic, repeated patterns, opportunities to extract helpers or shared utilities, near-identical code blocks. ([ihmeuw/vivarium-suite](https://github.com/ihmeuw/vivarium-suite/blob/HEAD/tools/ai-tools-public/agents/_review_dry.md) · ★4)
- **_review_maintainability**: Use when: reviewing code for maintainability, readability, documentation quality, implicit assumptions, magic numbers, docstring accuracy, coupling between components. ([ihmeuw/vivarium-suite](https://github.com/ihmeuw/vivarium-suite/blob/HEAD/tools/ai-tools-public/agents/_review_maintainability.md) · ★4)
- **_review_scorer**: Use when: independently scoring a single code-review finding for confidence (0-100) against the verbatim rubric, so low-confidence findings can be filtered out before synthesis. Spawned one-per-finding by simsci:_review-core. ([ihmeuw/vivarium-suite](https://github.com/ihmeuw/vivarium-suite/blob/HEAD/tools/ai-tools-public/agents/_review_scorer.md) · ★4)
- **_review_tests**: Use when: reviewing code for test coverage, test quality, edge cases, and whether tests validate the intended behavior without being brittle. ([ihmeuw/vivarium-suite](https://github.com/ihmeuw/vivarium-suite/blob/HEAD/tools/ai-tools-public/agents/_review_tests.md) · ★4)
- **_reviewer**: Code reviewer that runs a parallel specialist army covering security, performance, maintainability, API contracts, data integrity, test coverage, and error handling. Trigger on code review, review, PR review, pull request, or review army. ([navox-labs/agents](https://github.com/navox-labs/agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md) · ★15)
- **_reviewer-base**: All reviewers run in a **strictly read-only context**. Reviewers must not mutate the working tree, the Git index, the repository refs, or any remote state. This rule applies to **every tool**, not just `Edit`/`Write`. ([B16B1RD/cc-rite-workflow](https://github.com/B16B1RD/cc-rite-workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/rite/agents/_reviewer-base.md) · ★2)
- **_reviewer-base**: Shared base template for all code reviewer agents (Python, R, etc.). Defines the 10-lens review structure, report format, severity scale, and general instructions. Language-specific reviewers inherit from this template and override with domain-specific lenses. ([corocorohashioka/agec8027](https://github.com/corocorohashioka/agec8027/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_reviewer-base.md))
